{/* Generated by `python3 ui/journeys/tools/journeys.py docs --personas` in Teal-Connect/product from a recorded catalogue and the journey graphs. Do not edit: regenerate. */}

Every test persona of the mock catalogue, what to enter for it, and the journeys it lets you walk, both in
the hosted webapp and through the API. The personas are fictional: names, dates of birth, NI numbers,
postcodes, sign-in details and codes are test values, shown exactly as you enter them.

- **Inputs** are what you give the applicant's journey: the applicant's details when you create the user,
  and the sign-in, code or document at the source.
- **Source** is the part of the verification the persona exercises; **Behaviour** is what the source does
  for it, in the journey graph's vocabulary.
- Each persona lists the journeys it supports and the paths through them: a path is a variation (`v1`,
  `v2`, ...) or a planned walk of the journey graph. A persona is listed when it meets the path's
  requirements and one applicant can walk it; **resolver pick** marks the persona our own walks use, and
  **with** names the other personas of the same applicant on the path.
- **Webapp journeys** below give each path's screens in order. **API journeys** give its calls in order
  (method and path), the waits (a poll or a webhook) and the browser hand-offs. **Settings** are the user
  or client settings the path needs.
- Webhook deliveries and the callback redirect arrive at your own webhook receiver and callback URL.

- Catalogue: `staging-lucho`, read `2026-11-16T19:50:25Z` from `/qa/personas`, deployed build `payroll-api and mock-provider 5190356c`.
- Journey graphs and generator: `Teal-Connect/product@3b3356d5fa718fa935beb86ab8ad95a8b5a40719` (`ui/journeys/`, generated by `python3 ui/journeys/tools/journeys.py docs --personas`).

## Direct Connection

Direct Connection: the payroll lookup on the applicant's details (IRIS, Sage mock provider).

### `direct-director`

Source: Direct Connection; outcome `succeeded`; mock `mock-direct-provider`; fidelity `generated_mock`.

Behaviour: `trait.direct.employers=2`, `trait.direct.flags=director`, `trait.direct.history=covered`, `trait.direct.incomeFound=true`, `trait.direct.match=details`, `trait.direct.outcome=succeeded`, `trait.direct.payFrequency=monthly`.

| Input | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Daniel Shaw |
| Date of birth | 1975-02-25 |
| NI number | QQ100017A |
| Postcode | GU1 3XL |
| Direct providers | iris,sage |

**Webapp journeys**

- **Direct Connection: the silent lookup finds the applicant's payroll** (`direct-match`): `direct-match/v1`, `most-common`

**API journeys**

- **A server-side integrator follows the journey with its API key alone** (`api-key-state-reads`): `api-key-state-reads/v1`, `read-state-summary`
- **Direct Connection over the API: the lookup finds the applicant's payroll** (`direct-match`): `direct-match/v1`, `no-gaps`

**Webapp journeys**

- **The applicant leaves without finishing** (`abandons`): `abandons/v2` (resolver pick), `leave-after-upload` (resolver pick); `abandons/v4` (resolver pick), `leave-after-rejected-signin` (resolver pick) with `scraper-wrong-then-right-password`; `abandons/v5` (resolver pick), `leave-on-result` (resolver pick) with `scraper-full-window-nomfa`
- **No core income source: the applicant shares bank statements** (`bank-statements-only`): `bank-statements-only/v1` (resolver pick), `bank-statements-only/v2` (resolver pick), `most-common` (resolver pick), `bank-as-source` (resolver pick) with `bank-salary`
- **The applicant cancels, or a failed connection is reported to the client** (`cancel-or-failure-reported`): `cancel-or-failure-reported/v1` (resolver pick), `dismiss-confirmed` (resolver pick); `cancel-or-failure-reported/v2` (resolver pick), `connect-pick-employer` (resolver pick) with `oauth-provider-error.newpayroll`
- **Direct Connection rejects a mismatched identity** (`direct-identity-mismatch`): `direct-identity-mismatch/v1` (resolver pick), `most-common` (resolver pick)
- **Documents show no income: the client reviews the application** (`no-income-found`): `no-income-found/v1` (resolver pick), `no-income-found/v2` (resolver pick), `most-common` (resolver pick), `no-callback` (resolver pick) with `bank-no-salary`
- **No match and no method left: the client reviews the application** (`no-match-no-method`): `no-match-no-method/v1` (resolver pick), `no-source-on` (resolver pick); `no-match-no-method/v2` (resolver pick), `precheck-no-match` (resolver pick) with `employer-not-listed`

### `payslips-cover-window`

Source: Documents; outcome `read`; mock `deterministic-documents`; fidelity `generated_mock`.

Behaviour: `trait.documents.gapPeriods=0`, `trait.documents.payFrequency=monthly`, `trait.documents.reading=read`, `trait.documents.type=payslips`, `trait.payslips.coverWindow=true`.

| Input | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Document | payslips (payslip-*.pdf) |
| Document generator | ./gradlew :api:generateDocumentFixtures --applicant Alice Fixture --payslip-count 3 |
| Document window | 3 |

**Webapp journeys**

- **HMRC connection fails: the applicant is told and uses another way** (`hmrc-failure`): `hmrc-failure/v1` (resolver pick), `most-common` (resolver pick) with an applicant the Direct Connection mock does not hold, `hmrc-employed-authorised`; `hmrc-popup` (resolver pick) with an applicant the Direct Connection mock does not hold, `hmrc-employed-failed`

## Failures

Failures: a source that rejects, fails or cannot read what the applicant gives it.

### `api-gaps-check-fails.brightpaycloud`

Source: Failures; outcome `succeeded (gaps_check_error)`; mock `mock-gaps-check-failure`; fidelity `recorded_mock`.

Behaviour: `trait.payroll.failure=gaps_check_error`, `trait.payroll.mfa=false`, `trait.payroll.outcome=succeeded`, `trait.payroll.provider=scraper`, `trait.payroll.signInRejections=0`.

| Input | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Employer | Accuride International |
| Payroll provider | BrightPayCloud |
| Sign-in username | api-gaps-check-fails |
| Sign-in password | persona-password |
| requires_api_setting | GAPS_CHECK_FAILURE_PERSONA_ENABLED |

**Webapp journeys**

- **The applicant leaves without finishing** (`abandons`): `abandons/v5`, `leave-on-result` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **The applicant resets their payroll password and signs in** (`password-reset`): `password-reset/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **Rejected sign-in: the applicant stays on sign-in, retries and connects** (`rejected-signin-retry`): `rejected-signin-retry/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **User Connection: the applicant connects their payroll account** (`uc-connect`): `uc-connect/v1`, `uc-connect/v3`, `uc-connect/v5` (resolver pick), `uc-connect/v7` (resolver pick), `most-common`, `connect-pick-provider`, `gaps-check-fails` (resolver pick), `connect-pick-provider-gaps-check-fails` (resolver pick) with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **User Connection with missing pay periods: the applicant fills them or says there is no more** (`uc-gaps`): `uc-gaps/v2`, `result-summary` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **Employer not listed: the applicant adds it and connects payroll** (`unlisted-employer-payroll`): `unlisted-employer-payroll/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`

**API journeys**

- **A transient error over the API: the client retries** (`journey-error-retry`): `journey-error-retry/v1` (resolver pick), `read-fails` (resolver pick), `read-fails-retried` (resolver pick) with `direct-mismatched-identity`; `journey-error-retry/v2`, `create-account-unanswered` with `idempotency.answer-lost`, `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **Rejected sign-in over the API: the client asks again and the applicant connects** (`rejected-signin-retry`): `rejected-signin-retry/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **User Connection over the API: the client connects the applicant's payroll account** (`uc-connect`): `uc-connect/v1`, `uc-connect/v4` (resolver pick), `create-account-credentials-then-read-payroll-gaps`, `create-account-credentials-then-read-payroll-gaps-fails` (resolver pick) with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **User Connection with missing pay periods over the API: the applicant fills them or says there is no more** (`uc-gaps`): `uc-gaps/v2`, `read-state-summary` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **Employer not listed over the API: the applicant picks the provider and connects** (`unlisted-employer-payroll`): `unlisted-employer-payroll/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`

### `scraper-password-change-required.adpihcm`

Source: Failures; outcome `succeeded (password_change_required)`; mock `mock-scraper-failures`; fidelity `recorded_mock`.

Behaviour: `trait.payroll.failure=password_change_required`, `trait.payroll.mfa=false`, `trait.payroll.outcome=succeeded`, `trait.payroll.provider=scraper`, `trait.payroll.signInRejections=0`.

| Input | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Payroll provider | adpihcm |
| Sign-in username | scraper-password-change-required |
| Sign-in password | persona-password |

**Webapp journeys**

- **The applicant leaves without finishing** (`abandons`): `abandons/v5`, `leave-on-result` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **The applicant resets their payroll password and signs in** (`password-reset`): `password-reset/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`
- **Rejected sign-in: the applicant stays on sign-in, retries and connects** (`rejected-signin-retry`): `rejected-signin-retry/v1`, `most-common` with `direct-mismatched-identity`

## API journeys

The calls of each path in order (method and path), with the waits and the browser hand-offs.

### A server-side integrator follows the journey with its API key alone

Journey `api-key-state-reads`.

- `api-key-state-reads/v1`: `POST /users` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `consent` → `POST /authorisations` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `ninCapture` → `POST /accounts/direct-connections` → webhook `user-verification-state-changed` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `summary` → `GET /payroll/{user_id}`. Personas: `direct-ni-only-match` (resolver pick), `direct-director`, `direct-fortnightly-covered`, `direct-fourweekly-covered`, `direct-job-change`, `direct-low-income`, `direct-monthly-covered`, `direct-non-gbp`, `direct-two-employers`, `direct-weekly-covered`, `direct-zero-net`, `sage-bei-only-employment`.
- `api-key-state-reads/v2`: `POST /users` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `consent` → `POST /authorisations` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `ninCapture` → `POST /accounts/direct-connections` → webhook `user-verification-state-changed` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `payrollGapsOptions` → `GET /webapp/payroll/{user_id}/gaps` → `PUT /verification-state/{user_id}/no-more-information` says `summary` → `GET /payroll/{user_id}`. Personas: `direct-gap-middle` (resolver pick), `direct-gap-recent`, `direct-leaver`, `direct-short-tenure`.
- `read-state-gaps` (walk): `POST /users` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `consent` → `POST /authorisations` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `ninCapture` → `POST /accounts/direct-connections` → webhook `user-verification-state-changed` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `payrollGapsOptions` → `GET /webapp/payroll/{user_id}/gaps` → `PUT /verification-state/{user_id}/no-more-information` says `summary` → `GET /payroll/{user_id}`. Personas: `direct-gap-middle` (resolver pick), `direct-gap-recent`, `direct-leaver`, `direct-short-tenure`.
- `read-state-summary` (walk): `POST /users` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `consent` → `POST /authorisations` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `ninCapture` → `POST /accounts/direct-connections` → webhook `user-verification-state-changed` → `GET /verification-state/{user_id}` says `summary` → `GET /payroll/{user_id}`. Personas: `direct-ni-only-match` (resolver pick), `direct-director`, `direct-fortnightly-covered`, `direct-fourweekly-covered`, `direct-job-change`, `direct-low-income`, `direct-monthly-covered`, `direct-non-gbp`, `direct-two-employers`, `direct-weekly-covered`, `direct-zero-net`, `sage-bei-only-employment`.
